{"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_018RWs3NDRyyCGq9uaBEuMvk","type":"tool_result","content":"1\t/**\n2\t * Platform-wide invariant — BILLING-LOW-004:\n3\t *\n4\t * The Stripe webhook controller MUST carry `@Public()` so the\n5\t * upstream guard chain (ServiceIdentityGuard, JwtAuthGuard,\n6\t * TenantGuard, etc.) is skipped on the webhook path. The webhook's\n7\t * authenticity comes from Stripe's signature header verification\n8\t * which the controller performs in-handler — no platform guard\n9\t * applies because the request originates outside the platform's\n10\t * mTLS service-mesh.\n11\t *\n12\t * # Why this lives in tests/invariants/\n13\t *\n14\t * The current setup works correctly because:\n15\t *\n16\t *   1. The controller method has `@Public()`.\n17\t *   2. The platform guard chain respects the `@Public()` metadata\n18\t *      (every guard's canActivate() short-circuits when the\n19\t *      reflector finds IS_PUBLIC_KEY).\n20\t *\n21\t * Both are independently fragile:\n22\t *\n23\t *   - A future maintainer \"tidying up\" by removing `@Public()`\n24\t *     thinking it's unused would silently break every Stripe\n25\t *     webhook.\n26\t *   - A future maintainer adding a NEW global guard that doesn't\n27\t *     respect `@Public()` (e.g. a global rate-limit guard, a\n28\t *     mandatory tenant-scoped guard) would silently block every\n29\t *     Stripe webhook.\n30\t *\n31\t * This Tier-3 invariant catches the first regression class at PR\n32\t * review by asserting `@Public()` is present on the @Post()\n33\t * webhook handler. The second regression class (rogue global\n34\t * guard) is too broad for source-level detection — that one\n35\t * needs an e2e test (covered by the BILLING-LOW-004 follow-on\n36\t * tracked in the cure commit body).\n37\t *\n38\t * # What this spec asserts\n39\t *\n40\t *   1. StripeWebhookController has `@Public()` on the\n41\t *      handlePaymentWebhook method (the only @Post handler).\n42\t *   2. The controller imports `@Public()` from the canonical\n43\t *      location (libs/backend-common/src/auth/public.decorator.ts\n44\t *      or wherever the platform's IS_PUBLIC_KEY reflector key\n45\t *      lives), not a local one-off declaration that wouldn't\n46\t *      participate in the guard-chain bypass.\n47\t *\n48\t * Closes: docs/reviews/billing-expert/2026-04-28-core-platform-review.md#BILLING-LOW-004\n49\t */\n50\t\n51\timport { readFileSync } from 'node:fs';\n52\timport { resolve } from 'node:path';\n53\t\n54\tconst REPO_ROOT = resolve(__dirname, '..', '..');\n55\t\n56\tconst STRIPE_WEBHOOK_CONTROLLER_PATH =\n57\t  'apps/billing-service/src/billing/controllers/stripe-webhook.controller.ts';\n58\t\n59\tfunction read(rel: string): string {\n60\t  return readFileSync(resolve(REPO_ROOT, rel), 'utf8');\n61\t}\n62\t\n63\tdescribe('BILLING-LOW-004 — Stripe webhook public-path invariant', () => {\n64\t  it('StripeWebhookController has @Public() on the @Post webhook handler', () => {\n65\t    const src = read(STRIPE_WEBHOOK_CONTROLLER_PATH);\n66\t\n67\t    // The @Public() decorator MUST sit between the @Post() and\n68\t    // the method declaration. Permissive multiline matcher: any\n69\t    // amount of whitespace / comments between the two.\n70\t    const hasPublicBeforePost =\n71\t      /@Public\\s*\\(\\s*\\)\\s*[\\s\\S]{0,200}?@Post\\s*\\(/.test(src) ||\n72\t      /@Post\\s*\\([\\s\\S]{0,200}?@Public\\s*\\(\\s*\\)/.test(src);\n73\t    expect(hasPublicBeforePost).toBe(true);\n74\t  });\n75\t\n76\t  it('@Public is imported from the canonical platform location (not a local one-off)', () => {\n77\t    const src = read(STRIPE_WEBHOOK_CONTROLLER_PATH);\n78\t\n79\t    // Accept any of these canonical sources — the platform has\n80\t    // historically housed @Public in different locations and\n81\t    // they all consult the same reflector key. Any LOCAL\n82\t    // declaration of @Public() inside the file would fail the\n83\t    // reflector lookup and let guards run; we forbid that.\n84\t    const importsCanonicalPublic =\n85\t      /@aquaculture\\/backend-common[^'\"]*['\"]\\s*[\\s\\S]*?\\bPublic\\b/.test(src) ||\n86\t      /from\\s*['\"][^'\"]*\\/decorators\\/public[^'\"]*['\"]/.test(src) ||\n87\t      /from\\s*['\"][^'\"]*\\/auth\\/public[^'\"]*['\"]/.test(src) ||\n88\t      /from\\s*['\"]\\.\\.\\/auth\\/public\\.decorator['\"]/.test(src);\n89\t    expect(importsCanonicalPublic).toBe(true);\n90\t\n91\t    // Negative: assert there is no LOCAL `export const Public =`\n92\t    // or `function Public(` declaration in the file. Such a\n93\t    // local would shadow the platform decorator and silently\n94\t    // break the guard-chain bypass.\n95\t    expect(src).not.toMatch(/(?:export\\s+)?(?:const|function)\\s+Public\\s*[=(]/);\n96\t  });\n97\t\n98\t  it('StripeWebhookController controller path resolves to /webhooks/stripe (canonical)', () => {\n99\t    const src = read(STRIPE_WEBHOOK_CONTROLLER_PATH);\n100\t    // The controller decorator carries 'webhooks' and the @Post\n101\t    // route carries 'stripe' — concatenated to '/webhooks/stripe'.\n102\t    // Hard-coding the pair here means a future \"rename to\n103\t    // /api/webhooks/stripe\" silently breaks the deployed Stripe\n104\t    // dashboard webhook URL — the change would have to update\n105\t    // this invariant in the same PR, forcing reviewer awareness.\n106\t    expect(src).toMatch(/@Controller\\s*\\(\\s*['\"]webhooks['\"]\\s*\\)/);\n107\t    expect(src).toMatch(/@Post\\s*\\(\\s*['\"]stripe['\"]\\s*\\)/);\n108\t  });\n109\t});\n110\t"}]},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"5a4c4f70-f8bc-4bf7-9998-cc5c20f832fa","uuid":"a62486ee-7125-4bef-bc2d-aa64a2ffb668","timestamp":"2026-09-19T15:26:10.591Z","tool_use_result":{"type":"text","file":{"filePath":"/home/gharunner/actions-runner/_work/aquaculture_platform/aquaculture_platform/aria-worktrees/req-AIR-aria-evidence-judge-a4a4db32a408/tests/invariants/webhook-public-paths.spec.ts","content":"/**\n * Platform-wide invariant — BILLING-LOW-004:\n *\n * The Stripe webhook controller MUST carry `@Public()` so the\n * upstream guard chain (ServiceIdentityGuard, JwtAuthGuard,\n * TenantGuard, etc.) is skipped on the webhook path. The webhook's\n * authenticity comes from Stripe's signature header verification\n * which the controller performs in-handler — no platform guard\n * applies because the request originates outside the platform's\n * mTLS service-mesh.\n *\n * # Why this lives in tests/invariants/\n *\n * The current setup works correctly because:\n *\n *   1. The controller method has `@Public()`.\n *   2. The platform guard chain respects the `@Public()` metadata\n *      (every guard's canActivate() short-circuits when the\n *      reflector finds IS_PUBLIC_KEY).\n *\n * Both are independently fragile:\n *\n *   - A future maintainer \"tidying up\" by removing `@Public()`\n *     thinking it's unused would silently break every Stripe\n *     webhook.\n *   - A future maintainer adding a NEW global guard that doesn't\n *     respect `@Public()` (e.g. a global rate-limit guard, a\n *     mandatory tenant-scoped guard) would silently block every\n *     Stripe webhook.\n *\n * This Tier-3 invariant catches the first regression class at PR\n * review by asserting `@Public()` is present on the @Post()\n * webhook handler. The second regression class (rogue global\n * guard) is too broad for source-level detection — that one\n * needs an e2e test (covered by the BILLING-LOW-004 follow-on\n * tracked in the cure commit body).\n *\n * # What this spec asserts\n *\n *   1. StripeWebhookController has `@Public()` on the\n *      handlePaymentWebhook method (the only @Post handler).\n *   2. The controller imports `@Public()` from the canonical\n *      location (libs/backend-common/src/auth/public.decorator.ts\n *      or wherever the platform's IS_PUBLIC_KEY reflector key\n *      lives), not a local one-off declaration that wouldn't\n *      participate in the guard-chain bypass.\n *\n * Closes: docs/reviews/billing-expert/2026-04-28-core-platform-review.md#BILLING-LOW-004\n */\n\nimport { readFileSync } from 'node:fs';\nimport { resolve } from 'node:path';\n\nconst REPO_ROOT = resolve(__dirname, '..', '..');\n\nconst STRIPE_WEBHOOK_CONTROLLER_PATH =\n  'apps/billing-service/src/billing/controllers/stripe-webhook.controller.ts';\n\nfunction read(rel: string): string {\n  return readFileSync(resolve(REPO_ROOT, rel), 'utf8');\n}\n\ndescribe('BILLING-LOW-004 — Stripe webhook public-path invariant', () => {\n  it('StripeWebhookController has @Public() on the @Post webhook handler', () => {\n    const src = read(STRIPE_WEBHOOK_CONTROLLER_PATH);\n\n    // The @Public() decorator MUST sit between the @Post() and\n    // the method declaration. Permissive multiline matcher: any\n    // amount of whitespace / comments between the two.\n    const hasPublicBeforePost =\n      /@Public\\s*\\(\\s*\\)\\s*[\\s\\S]{0,200}?@Post\\s*\\(/.test(src) ||\n      /@Post\\s*\\([\\s\\S]{0,200}?@Public\\s*\\(\\s*\\)/.test(src);\n    expect(hasPublicBeforePost).toBe(true);\n  });\n\n  it('@Public is imported from the canonical platform location (not a local one-off)', () => {\n    const src = read(STRIPE_WEBHOOK_CONTROLLER_PATH);\n\n    // Accept any of these canonical sources — the platform has\n    // historically housed @Public in different locations and\n    // they all consult the same reflector key. Any LOCAL\n    // declaration of @Public() inside the file would fail the\n    // reflector lookup and let guards run; we forbid that.\n    const importsCanonicalPublic =\n      /@aquaculture\\/backend-common[^'\"]*['\"]\\s*[\\s\\S]*?\\bPublic\\b/.test(src) ||\n      /from\\s*['\"][^'\"]*\\/decorators\\/public[^'\"]*['\"]/.test(src) ||\n      /from\\s*['\"][^'\"]*\\/auth\\/public[^'\"]*['\"]/.test(src) ||\n      /from\\s*['\"]\\.\\.\\/auth\\/public\\.decorator['\"]/.test(src);\n    expect(importsCanonicalPublic).toBe(true);\n\n    // Negative: assert there is no LOCAL `export const Public =`\n    // or `function Public(` declaration in the file. Such a\n    // local would shadow the platform decorator and silently\n    // break the guard-chain bypass.\n    expect(src).not.toMatch(/(?:export\\s+)?(?:const|function)\\s+Public\\s*[=(]/);\n  });\n\n  it('StripeWebhookController controller path resolves to /webhooks/stripe (canonical)', () => {\n    const src = read(STRIPE_WEBHOOK_CONTROLLER_PATH);\n    // The controller decorator carries 'webhooks' and the @Post\n    // route carries 'stripe' — concatenated to '/webhooks/stripe'.\n    // Hard-coding the pair here means a future \"rename to\n    // /api/webhooks/stripe\" silently breaks the deployed Stripe\n    // dashboard webhook URL — the change would have to update\n    // this invariant in the same PR, forcing reviewer awareness.\n    expect(src).toMatch(/@Controller\\s*\\(\\s*['\"]webhooks['\"]\\s*\\)/);\n    expect(src).toMatch(/@Post\\s*\\(\\s*['\"]stripe['\"]\\s*\\)/);\n  });\n});\n","numLines":110,"startLine":1,"totalLines":110}}}

{"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_011x87kSHBta5uKrC8FG1Cc3","type":"tool_result","content":"520\t        return\n521\t    # ARIA self-output / runtime artefacts are NOT admissible evidence\n522\t    # for an agent claim (CLAUDE.md L1, Plan 016 §Agent output untrusted).\n523\t    if not allow_self_output and rel_str.startswith(SELF_OUTPUT_MARKERS):\n524\t        errors.append({\"code\": \"agent_evidence_self_output\", \"path\": rel_str})\n525\t        return\n526\t    if not absolute.exists() or not absolute.is_file():\n527\t        errors.append({\"code\": \"agent_evidence_path_missing\", \"path\": path})\n528\t        return\n529\t    checked.append(rel_str)\n530\t    if line is None:\n531\t        return\n532\t    if line <= 0:\n533\t        errors.append({\"code\": \"agent_evidence_line_invalid\", \"path\": path, \"line\": line})\n534\t        return\n535\t    line_count = len(absolute.read_text(encoding=\"utf-8\", errors=\"replace\").splitlines())\n536\t    if line > line_count:\n537\t        errors.append({\"code\": \"agent_evidence_line_missing\", \"path\": path, \"line\": line, \"line_count\": line_count})\n538\t\n539\t\n540\tdef validate_agent_response_evidence(\n541\t    *,\n542\t    response: dict[str, Any],\n543\t    workspace_root: str | Path,\n544\t    request: dict[str, Any] | None = None,\n545\t    probe_session: GitProbeSession | None = None,\n546\t) -> dict[str, Any]:\n547\t    \"\"\"Re-fetch every evidence ref claimed by an agent response and verify it exists in the repo.\n548\t\n549\t    Why: Plan 016 V-24 — agent output is data, not truth. The contract\n550\t    layer (agent_contract.validate_response) checks the SHAPE of the\n551\t    satisfaction matrix and the response envelope. This function checks\n552\t    that every evidence_ref the agent attached to its verdicts actually\n553\t    points at a real file (and line, when given) at the workspace SHA.\n554\t    A response that passes both gates is contractually safe to publish\n555\t    as a CONVERGED plan / promoted finding.\n556\t\n557\t    Returns: {\n558\t        \"valid\": bool,\n559\t        \"errors\": [{\"code\": ..., \"path\": ..., ...}],\n560\t        \"checked_refs\": [<sorted unique paths>],\n561\t    }\n562\t    \"\"\"\n563\t    root = Path(workspace_root).resolve()\n564\t    errors: list[dict[str, Any]] = []\n565\t    checked: list[str] = []\n566\t    evidence_envelopes: list[dict[str, Any]] = []\n567\t    # ORPHAN-CRITICAL-734 — role-scoped, not global. Only a role whose\n568\t    # SUBJECT is another agent's verdict may cite that verdict's artifact,\n569\t    # and only when the results ledger still vouches for its bytes.\n570\t    allow_kernel_artifacts = str(\n571\t        (request or {}).get(\"role\") or \"\",\n572\t    ) in ARBITRATION_ROLES\n573\t    # ONE probe session for the whole submission (evidence_probe): the\n574\t    # baseline is resolved once for all refs, and every git probe of this\n575\t    # decision shares one liveness clock — the seam that used to hand each\n576\t    # ref a 5 s budget and re-dispatched the whole paid run on one stall.\n577\t    # The submit decision passes the session it already resolved its\n578\t    # evidence target on, so a target git could not read is remembered here\n579\t    # and every ref grades `verification_unavailable` rather than being\n580\t    # compared against an anchor the submitter did not cite.\n581\t    probe_session = probe_session if probe_session is not None else GitProbeSession()\n582\t\n583\t    response_refs = response.get(\"evidence_refs\") or []\n584\t    if not isinstance(response_refs, list):\n585\t        errors.append({\"code\": \"agent_evidence_refs_not_list\"})\n586\t        response_refs = []\n587\t    for ref in response_refs:\n588\t        if not isinstance(ref, str) or not ref.strip():\n589\t            errors.append({\"code\": \"agent_evidence_ref_not_string\"})\n590\t            continue\n591\t        _check_agent_ref(\n592\t            ref, root=root, errors=errors, checked=checked,\n593\t            allow_kernel_artifacts=allow_kernel_artifacts,\n594\t        )\n595\t        if _is_ledger_pointer_ref(ref):\n596\t            continue  # Z2 — a ledger pointer has no repo classification\n597\t        if allow_kernel_artifacts and _kernel_artifact_verdict(ref, root)[0]:\n598\t            # Verified by content hash against the results ledger — the\n599\t            # repo classification below cannot describe it (the artifact\n600\t            # is state-store-owned and was never meant to be committed).\n601\t            continue\n602\t        envelope = classify_evidence_ref(\n603\t            ref,\n604\t            workspace_root=root,\n605\t            source_hint=\"agent_output\",\n606\t            target_sha=_request_target_sha(request),\n607\t            probe_session=probe_session,\n608\t        )\n609\t        evidence_envelopes.append(envelope.to_dict())\n610\t        try:\n611\t            EvidencePolicy.require_repo_verified(envelope)\n612\t        except GovernanceError as exc:\n613\t            errors.append({\n614\t                \"code\": _unverified_evidence_code(\"agent\", envelope.trust_grade),\n615\t                \"ref\": ref,\n616\t                \"reason\": str(exc),\n617\t            })\n618\t\n619\t    # Plan 024 §B-2 — satisfaction_matrix non-empty enforcement.\n620\t    # Pre-fix an agent response with `satisfaction_matrix: []` passed\n621\t    # consensus because the matrix-iteration loop simply never ran.\n622\t    # Combined with the empty-allowed_scope skip (line below), a judge\n623\t    # could rubber-stamp a request with zero criteria checked. The\n624\t    # request can opt out via `allow_empty_satisfaction_matrix=True`\n625\t    # for the rare judge-only domain that explicitly does not produce\n626\t    # per-criterion evidence — operator-approval-gated; not a default.\n627\t    matrix = response.get(\"satisfaction_matrix\") or []\n628\t    allow_empty_matrix = bool(request and request.get(\"allow_empty_satisfaction_matrix\"))\n629\t    if not matrix and not allow_empty_matrix:\n630\t        errors.append({\"code\": \"evidence_satisfaction_matrix_must_be_non_empty\"})\n631\t    if isinstance(matrix, list):\n632\t        for entry in matrix:\n633\t            if not isinstance(entry, dict):\n634\t                continue\n635\t            entry_refs = entry.get(\"evidence_refs\") or []\n636\t            if not isinstance(entry_refs, list):\n637\t                errors.append({\"code\": \"agent_matrix_evidence_refs_not_list\", \"id\": entry.get(\"id\")})\n638\t                continue\n639\t            for ref in entry_refs:\n640\t                if not isinstance(ref, str) or not ref.strip():\n641\t                    errors.append(\n642\t                        {\"code\": \"agent_matrix_evidence_ref_not_string\", \"id\": entry.get(\"id\")}\n643\t                    )\n644\t                    continue\n645\t                _check_agent_ref(\n646\t                    ref, root=root, errors=errors, checked=checked,\n647\t                    allow_kernel_artifacts=allow_kernel_artifacts,\n648\t                )\n649\t                if _is_ledger_pointer_ref(ref):\n650\t                    continue  # Z2 — same single definition as above\n651\t                if allow_kernel_artifacts and _kernel_artifact_verdict(ref, root)[0]:\n652\t                    continue  # ORPHAN-734 — same single definition as above\n653\t                envelope = classify_evidence_ref(\n654\t                    ref,\n655\t                    workspace_root=root,\n656\t                    source_hint=\"agent_output\",\n657\t                    target_sha=_request_target_sha(request),\n658\t                    probe_session=probe_session,\n659\t                )\n660\t                evidence_envelopes.append(envelope.to_dict())\n661\t                try:\n662\t                    EvidencePolicy.require_repo_verified(envelope)\n663\t                except GovernanceError as exc:\n664\t                    errors.append({\n665\t                        \"code\": _unverified_evidence_code(\"agent\", envelope.trust_grade),\n666\t                        \"ref\": ref,\n667\t                        \"reason\": str(exc),\n668\t                    })\n669\t\n670\t    # Cross-check: when a request is provided, every ref the agent\n671\t    # claims must either live inside `allowed_scope` OR be one of the\n672\t    # request's evidence_refs (the canonical bounding box). Refs\n673\t    # outside the box are rejected as scope leakage.\n674\t    #\n675\t    # Plan 024 §B-2 — allowed_scope MUST be provided when a request is\n676\t    # supplied. Pre-fix the empty-list case skipped scope enforcement\n677\t    # entirely under \"preserves backward compatibility with legacy\n678\t    # aria/agent-invocation-request/v1 rows that pre-date Plan 016\".\n679\t    # That path is now closed: legacy rows are caught at\n680\t    # _strict_request_view (line ~964); by the time validate_agent_-\n681\t    # response_evidence sees the request the strict fields are\n682\t    # present.\n683\t    if request is not None:\n684\t        if \"allowed_scope\" not in request:\n685\t            errors.append({\"code\": \"evidence_request_missing_allowed_scope\"})\n686\t        else:\n687\t            allowed_globs = list(request.get(\"allowed_scope\") or [])\n688\t            allowed_request_refs = {\n689\t                (_parse_agent_ref(r) or (\"\", None))[0]\n690\t                for r in (request.get(\"evidence_refs\") or [])\n691\t                if isinstance(r, str)\n692\t            }\n693\t            for path in checked:\n694\t                if _is_ledger_pointer_ref(path):\n695\t                    continue  # Z2 — pointer identity is bound at mint, not by glob\n696\t                if allow_kernel_artifacts and _kernel_artifact_verdict(path, root)[0]:\n697\t                    # ORPHAN-734 — an arbitrated artifact's identity is bound\n698\t                    # by the results ledger's content hash, not by a repo glob\n699\t                    # (the same reason the pointer above skips this check).\n700\t                    continue\n701\t                if path in allowed_request_refs:\n702\t                    continue\n703\t                if not _path_matches_any_glob(path, allowed_globs):\n704\t                    errors.append(\n705\t                        {\n706\t                            \"code\": \"agent_evidence_outside_allowed_scope\",\n707\t                            \"path\": path,\n708\t                            \"allowed_scope\": allowed_globs,\n709\t                        }\n710\t                    )\n711\t\n712\t    return {\n713\t        \"valid\": not errors,\n714\t        \"errors\": errors,\n715\t        \"checked_refs\": sorted(set(checked)),\n716\t        \"evidence_envelopes\": evidence_envelopes,\n717\t    }\n718\t\n719\t"}]},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"5a4c4f70-f8bc-4bf7-9998-cc5c20f832fa","uuid":"91b71649-dd3f-4af6-a8d4-30b2641fbaf5","timestamp":"2026-09-19T15:27:09.615Z","tool_use_result":{"type":"text","file":{"filePath":"/home/gharunner/actions-runner/_work/aquaculture_platform/aquaculture_platform/aria-worktrees/req-AIR-aria-evidence-judge-a4a4db32a408/aria-kernel/aria_kernel/evidence_validator.py","content":"        return\n    # ARIA self-output / runtime artefacts are NOT admissible evidence\n    # for an agent claim (CLAUDE.md L1, Plan 016 §Agent output untrusted).\n    if not allow_self_output and rel_str.startswith(SELF_OUTPUT_MARKERS):\n        errors.append({\"code\": \"agent_evidence_self_output\", \"path\": rel_str})\n        return\n    if not absolute.exists() or not absolute.is_file():\n        errors.append({\"code\": \"agent_evidence_path_missing\", \"path\": path})\n        return\n    checked.append(rel_str)\n    if line is None:\n        return\n    if line <= 0:\n        errors.append({\"code\": \"agent_evidence_line_invalid\", \"path\": path, \"line\": line})\n        return\n    line_count = len(absolute.read_text(encoding=\"utf-8\", errors=\"replace\").splitlines())\n    if line > line_count:\n        errors.append({\"code\": \"agent_evidence_line_missing\", \"path\": path, \"line\": line, \"line_count\": line_count})\n\n\ndef validate_agent_response_evidence(\n    *,\n    response: dict[str, Any],\n    workspace_root: str | Path,\n    request: dict[str, Any] | None = None,\n    probe_session: GitProbeSession | None = None,\n) -> dict[str, Any]:\n    \"\"\"Re-fetch every evidence ref claimed by an agent response and verify it exists in the repo.\n\n    Why: Plan 016 V-24 — agent output is data, not truth. The contract\n    layer (agent_contract.validate_response) checks the SHAPE of the\n    satisfaction matrix and the response envelope. This function checks\n    that every evidence_ref the agent attached to its verdicts actually\n    points at a real file (and line, when given) at the workspace SHA.\n    A response that passes both gates is contractually safe to publish\n    as a CONVERGED plan / promoted finding.\n\n    Returns: {\n        \"valid\": bool,\n        \"errors\": [{\"code\": ..., \"path\": ..., ...}],\n        \"checked_refs\": [<sorted unique paths>],\n    }\n    \"\"\"\n    root = Path(workspace_root).resolve()\n    errors: list[dict[str, Any]] = []\n    checked: list[str] = []\n    evidence_envelopes: list[dict[str, Any]] = []\n    # ORPHAN-CRITICAL-734 — role-scoped, not global. Only a role whose\n    # SUBJECT is another agent's verdict may cite that verdict's artifact,\n    # and only when the results ledger still vouches for its bytes.\n    allow_kernel_artifacts = str(\n        (request or {}).get(\"role\") or \"\",\n    ) in ARBITRATION_ROLES\n    # ONE probe session for the whole submission (evidence_probe): the\n    # baseline is resolved once for all refs, and every git probe of this\n    # decision shares one liveness clock — the seam that used to hand each\n    # ref a 5 s budget and re-dispatched the whole paid run on one stall.\n    # The submit decision passes the session it already resolved its\n    # evidence target on, so a target git could not read is remembered here\n    # and every ref grades `verification_unavailable` rather than being\n    # compared against an anchor the submitter did not cite.\n    probe_session = probe_session if probe_session is not None else GitProbeSession()\n\n    response_refs = response.get(\"evidence_refs\") or []\n    if not isinstance(response_refs, list):\n        errors.append({\"code\": \"agent_evidence_refs_not_list\"})\n        response_refs = []\n    for ref in response_refs:\n        if not isinstance(ref, str) or not ref.strip():\n            errors.append({\"code\": \"agent_evidence_ref_not_string\"})\n            continue\n        _check_agent_ref(\n            ref, root=root, errors=errors, checked=checked,\n            allow_kernel_artifacts=allow_kernel_artifacts,\n        )\n        if _is_ledger_pointer_ref(ref):\n            continue  # Z2 — a ledger pointer has no repo classification\n        if allow_kernel_artifacts and _kernel_artifact_verdict(ref, root)[0]:\n            # Verified by content hash against the results ledger — the\n            # repo classification below cannot describe it (the artifact\n            # is state-store-owned and was never meant to be committed).\n            continue\n        envelope = classify_evidence_ref(\n            ref,\n            workspace_root=root,\n            source_hint=\"agent_output\",\n            target_sha=_request_target_sha(request),\n            probe_session=probe_session,\n        )\n        evidence_envelopes.append(envelope.to_dict())\n        try:\n            EvidencePolicy.require_repo_verified(envelope)\n        except GovernanceError as exc:\n            errors.append({\n                \"code\": _unverified_evidence_code(\"agent\", envelope.trust_grade),\n                \"ref\": ref,\n                \"reason\": str(exc),\n            })\n\n    # Plan 024 §B-2 — satisfaction_matrix non-empty enforcement.\n    # Pre-fix an agent response with `satisfaction_matrix: []` passed\n    # consensus because the matrix-iteration loop simply never ran.\n    # Combined with the empty-allowed_scope skip (line below), a judge\n    # could rubber-stamp a request with zero criteria checked. The\n    # request can opt out via `allow_empty_satisfaction_matrix=True`\n    # for the rare judge-only domain that explicitly does not produce\n    # per-criterion evidence — operator-approval-gated; not a default.\n    matrix = response.get(\"satisfaction_matrix\") or []\n    allow_empty_matrix = bool(request and request.get(\"allow_empty_satisfaction_matrix\"))\n    if not matrix and not allow_empty_matrix:\n        errors.append({\"code\": \"evidence_satisfaction_matrix_must_be_non_empty\"})\n    if isinstance(matrix, list):\n        for entry in matrix:\n            if not isinstance(entry, dict):\n                continue\n            entry_refs = entry.get(\"evidence_refs\") or []\n            if not isinstance(entry_refs, list):\n                errors.append({\"code\": \"agent_matrix_evidence_refs_not_list\", \"id\": entry.get(\"id\")})\n                continue\n            for ref in entry_refs:\n                if not isinstance(ref, str) or not ref.strip():\n                    errors.append(\n                        {\"code\": \"agent_matrix_evidence_ref_not_string\", \"id\": entry.get(\"id\")}\n                    )\n                    continue\n                _check_agent_ref(\n                    ref, root=root, errors=errors, checked=checked,\n                    allow_kernel_artifacts=allow_kernel_artifacts,\n                )\n                if _is_ledger_pointer_ref(ref):\n                    continue  # Z2 — same single definition as above\n                if allow_kernel_artifacts and _kernel_artifact_verdict(ref, root)[0]:\n                    continue  # ORPHAN-734 — same single definition as above\n                envelope = classify_evidence_ref(\n                    ref,\n                    workspace_root=root,\n                    source_hint=\"agent_output\",\n                    target_sha=_request_target_sha(request),\n                    probe_session=probe_session,\n                )\n                evidence_envelopes.append(envelope.to_dict())\n                try:\n                    EvidencePolicy.require_repo_verified(envelope)\n                except GovernanceError as exc:\n                    errors.append({\n                        \"code\": _unverified_evidence_code(\"agent\", envelope.trust_grade),\n                        \"ref\": ref,\n                        \"reason\": str(exc),\n                    })\n\n    # Cross-check: when a request is provided, every ref the agent\n    # claims must either live inside `allowed_scope` OR be one of the\n    # request's evidence_refs (the canonical bounding box). Refs\n    # outside the box are rejected as scope leakage.\n    #\n    # Plan 024 §B-2 — allowed_scope MUST be provided when a request is\n    # supplied. Pre-fix the empty-list case skipped scope enforcement\n    # entirely under \"preserves backward compatibility with legacy\n    # aria/agent-invocation-request/v1 rows that pre-date Plan 016\".\n    # That path is now closed: legacy rows are caught at\n    # _strict_request_view (line ~964); by the time validate_agent_-\n    # response_evidence sees the request the strict fields are\n    # present.\n    if request is not None:\n        if \"allowed_scope\" not in request:\n            errors.append({\"code\": \"evidence_request_missing_allowed_scope\"})\n        else:\n            allowed_globs = list(request.get(\"allowed_scope\") or [])\n            allowed_request_refs = {\n                (_parse_agent_ref(r) or (\"\", None))[0]\n                for r in (request.get(\"evidence_refs\") or [])\n                if isinstance(r, str)\n            }\n            for path in checked:\n                if _is_ledger_pointer_ref(path):\n                    continue  # Z2 — pointer identity is bound at mint, not by glob\n                if allow_kernel_artifacts and _kernel_artifact_verdict(path, root)[0]:\n                    # ORPHAN-734 — an arbitrated artifact's identity is bound\n                    # by the results ledger's content hash, not by a repo glob\n                    # (the same reason the pointer above skips this check).\n                    continue\n                if path in allowed_request_refs:\n                    continue\n                if not _path_matches_any_glob(path, allowed_globs):\n                    errors.append(\n                        {\n                            \"code\": \"agent_evidence_outside_allowed_scope\",\n                            \"path\": path,\n                            \"allowed_scope\": allowed_globs,\n                        }\n                    )\n\n    return {\n        \"valid\": not errors,\n        \"errors\": errors,\n        \"checked_refs\": sorted(set(checked)),\n        \"evidence_envelopes\": evidence_envelopes,\n    }\n\n","numLines":200,"startLine":520,"totalLines":774}}}

{"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01FikgeV1GcNxJ7ZgiDvsaV6","type":"tool_result","content":"2225\t            contract = \"tools/aria-poc/ci_executor_contract_proven.md\"\n2226\t            raise ClaudeCliUnavailable(f\"{exc}; see {contract}\") from exc\n2227\t        raise\n2228\t    # Plan ARIA-V7 §2g v2 + V7.10 envelope-extraction fix.\n2229\t    #\n2230\t    # WHY: claude -p stream-json emits JSONL events\n2231\t    # ({\"result\": \"...\", \"total_cost_usd\": ..., ...}) where the agent's final message may contain the\n2232\t    # actual aria/agent-response/v1 envelope as fenced ```json``` block\n2233\t    # or as final JSON block. Writing raw JSONL to output_path means\n2234\t    # kernel submit-result reads runtime telemetry instead of the envelope,\n2235\t    # which fails:\n2236\t    #   response_schema: missing required fields:\n2237\t    #     ['$schema', 'request_id', 'claim_id', 'agent_id', 'role',\n2238\t    #      'status', 'satisfaction_matrix']\n2239\t    #\n2240\t    # HOW: parse JSONL, extract the final message, find the embedded\n2241\t    # envelope JSON, INJECT mandatory identity fields ($schema,\n2242\t    # request_id, claim_id, agent_id, role, status) from the known\n2243\t    # ci_executor context (these aren't the agent's job — the agent\n2244\t    # only knows its plan content), synthesize a satisfaction_matrix\n2245\t    # from must_satisfy with verdict=satisfied when the agent omitted\n2246\t    # it, then write the corrected envelope to output_path.\n2247\t    #\n2248\t    # Tier hierarchy: Tier-2 (Make it automatic) — the envelope shape\n2249\t    # is now produced correctly by default; agents don't have to know\n2250\t    # internal kernel identity fields.\n2251\t    if completed.stdout:\n2252\t        envelope = _build_envelope_from_claude_output(\n2253\t            raw_stdout=completed.stdout,\n2254\t            request_id=request_id,\n2255\t            claim_id=claim_id or \"\",\n2256\t            agent_id=agent_id or \"\",\n2257\t            role=role,\n2258\t            subagent_type=subagent_type,\n2259\t            must_satisfy=must_satisfy or [],\n2260\t            dispatch_model=agent_profile.model,\n2261\t        )\n2262\t        envelope[\"details\"][\"agent_contract_hash\"] = agent_contract.contract_hash\n2263\t        output_path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)\n2264\t        _write_sanitized_envelope(output_path, envelope)\n2265\t        resolved_transcript_path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)\n2266\t        resolved_transcript_path.write_text(completed.stdout, encoding=\"utf-8\")\n2267\t        # Plan ARIA-V3.1-D3 — per-LLM-call cost attribution. Gated on\n2268\t        # _MOCK_MODE_AT_ENTRY frozen sentinel (V3.1-D2) so a mid-run\n2269\t        # CLAUDE_CLI_MOCK flip cannot rewrite mock-mode classification\n2270\t        # between mint + record sites. request_envelope + tools_dir\n2271\t        # MUST both be supplied for the record to fire — None defaults\n2272\t        # preserve V8 backward-compat for callers that haven't migrated.\n2273\t        if (\n2274\t            completed.returncode == 0\n2275\t            and _MOCK_MODE_AT_ENTRY is False\n2276\t            and request_envelope is not None\n2277\t            and tools_dir is not None\n2278\t        ):\n2279\t            _record_claude_cli_usage(\n2280\t                raw_stdout=completed.stdout,\n2281\t                request_envelope=request_envelope,\n2282\t                tools_dir=tools_dir,\n2283\t                role=role,\n2284\t                request_id=request_id,"}]},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"5a4c4f70-f8bc-4bf7-9998-cc5c20f832fa","uuid":"efa42908-80b4-41af-b9ba-8523bd6e5ea6","timestamp":"2026-09-19T15:28:20.731Z","tool_use_result":{"type":"text","file":{"filePath":"/home/gharunner/actions-runner/_work/aquaculture_platform/aquaculture_platform/aria-worktrees/req-AIR-aria-evidence-judge-a4a4db32a408/tools/aria-poc/ci_executor.py","content":"            contract = \"tools/aria-poc/ci_executor_contract_proven.md\"\n            raise ClaudeCliUnavailable(f\"{exc}; see {contract}\") from exc\n        raise\n    # Plan ARIA-V7 §2g v2 + V7.10 envelope-extraction fix.\n    #\n    # WHY: claude -p stream-json emits JSONL events\n    # ({\"result\": \"...\", \"total_cost_usd\": ..., ...}) where the agent's final message may contain the\n    # actual aria/agent-response/v1 envelope as fenced ```json``` block\n    # or as final JSON block. Writing raw JSONL to output_path means\n    # kernel submit-result reads runtime telemetry instead of the envelope,\n    # which fails:\n    #   response_schema: missing required fields:\n    #     ['$schema', 'request_id', 'claim_id', 'agent_id', 'role',\n    #      'status', 'satisfaction_matrix']\n    #\n    # HOW: parse JSONL, extract the final message, find the embedded\n    # envelope JSON, INJECT mandatory identity fields ($schema,\n    # request_id, claim_id, agent_id, role, status) from the known\n    # ci_executor context (these aren't the agent's job — the agent\n    # only knows its plan content), synthesize a satisfaction_matrix\n    # from must_satisfy with verdict=satisfied when the agent omitted\n    # it, then write the corrected envelope to output_path.\n    #\n    # Tier hierarchy: Tier-2 (Make it automatic) — the envelope shape\n    # is now produced correctly by default; agents don't have to know\n    # internal kernel identity fields.\n    if completed.stdout:\n        envelope = _build_envelope_from_claude_output(\n            raw_stdout=completed.stdout,\n            request_id=request_id,\n            claim_id=claim_id or \"\",\n            agent_id=agent_id or \"\",\n            role=role,\n            subagent_type=subagent_type,\n            must_satisfy=must_satisfy or [],\n            dispatch_model=agent_profile.model,\n        )\n        envelope[\"details\"][\"agent_contract_hash\"] = agent_contract.contract_hash\n        output_path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)\n        _write_sanitized_envelope(output_path, envelope)\n        resolved_transcript_path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)\n        resolved_transcript_path.write_text(completed.stdout, encoding=\"utf-8\")\n        # Plan ARIA-V3.1-D3 — per-LLM-call cost attribution. Gated on\n        # _MOCK_MODE_AT_ENTRY frozen sentinel (V3.1-D2) so a mid-run\n        # CLAUDE_CLI_MOCK flip cannot rewrite mock-mode classification\n        # between mint + record sites. request_envelope + tools_dir\n        # MUST both be supplied for the record to fire — None defaults\n        # preserve V8 backward-compat for callers that haven't migrated.\n        if (\n            completed.returncode == 0\n            and _MOCK_MODE_AT_ENTRY is False\n            and request_envelope is not None\n            and tools_dir is not None\n        ):\n            _record_claude_cli_usage(\n                raw_stdout=completed.stdout,\n                request_envelope=request_envelope,\n                tools_dir=tools_dir,\n                role=role,\n                request_id=request_id,","numLines":60,"startLine":2225,"totalLines":5265}}}

{"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_011PSzimvh9NqRXUc287bsNv","type":"tool_result","content":"40\tfrom .tool_registry import GovernanceError, append_tools_governance, ensure_tools_dir, utc_now\n41\t\n42\t\n43\tdef is_judge_role(role: str | None) -> bool:\n44\t    return role in JUDGE_ROLES\n45\t\n46\t\n47\tdef is_supporting_role(role: str | None) -> bool:\n48\t    return role in SUPPORTING_ROLES\n49\t\n50\t\n51\tdef _coerce_severity(value: Any, *, fallback: str = \"medium\") -> str:\n52\t    \"\"\"feedback_store accepts {low, medium, high, critical}; agent envelope\n53\t    may use uppercase per finding schema. Normalize without rejecting.\"\"\"\n54\t    if not isinstance(value, str):\n55\t        return fallback\n56\t    normalized = value.strip().lower()\n57\t    if normalized in FEEDBACK_SEVERITIES:\n58\t        return normalized\n59\t    if normalized == \"informational\":\n60\t        return \"low\"\n61\t    return fallback\n62\t\n63\t\n64\tdef _verdict_field(details: dict[str, Any]) -> dict[str, Any]:\n65\t    \"\"\"The judge .md prompts shape verdicts under details.verdict (evidence,\n66\t    adversarial) or details.consensus (arbiter). Return whichever exists.\n67\t    \"\"\"\n68\t    if isinstance(details.get(\"verdict\"), dict):\n69\t        return dict(details[\"verdict\"])\n70\t    if isinstance(details.get(\"consensus\"), dict):\n71\t        return dict(details[\"consensus\"])\n72\t    if isinstance(details.get(\"verdict\"), str):\n73\t        # Flat shape (ORPHAN-HIGH-629 sibling): the judge contract file says\n74\t        # BOTH \"Return JSON with: verdict, judge_id, confidence...\" and\n75\t        # \"details.verdict.rationale\". The first live judge run answered\n76\t        # flat, was ACCEPTED by the envelope gate, and dropped HERE with\n77\t        # \"expected verdict ..., got None\" — a verdict lost between two\n78\t        # halves of one document. Honour both spellings; the envelope gate\n79\t        # stays the only authority on validity.\n80\t        return {\n81\t            key: details.get(key)\n82\t            for key in (\n83\t                \"verdict\", \"tool_id\", \"run_id\", \"finding_id\", \"judge_id\",\n84\t                \"model\", \"prompt_hash\", \"confidence\", \"rationale\",\n85\t                \"evidence_refs\", \"judgment_group_id\", \"severity\",\n86\t            )\n87\t            if details.get(key) is not None\n88\t        }\n89\t    return {}\n90\t\n91\t\n92\tdef validate_judge_response(\n93\t    *,\n94\t    request: dict[str, Any],\n95\t    response: dict[str, Any],\n96\t) -> list[str]:\n97\t    \"\"\"Y5 (ORPHAN-706) — the judge output contract, checkable BEFORE accept.\n98\t\n99\t    The second sealed night accepted 12 judge results whose envelopes never\n100\t    carried a verdict the bridge could read (\"expected verdict ..., got\n101\t    None\") — each one an accepted row that could never fold, burning bridge\n102\t    retries toward permanent_fail. This is the SINGLE definition of what a\n103\t    judge response must carry; ``record_judge_verdict_from_response`` raises\n104\t    on exactly this list, and the executor's pre-submit gate releases the\n105\t    claim on it instead of sealing an unfoldable result.\n106\t\n107\t    Returns [] for non-judge roles: the contract binds judges only.\n108\t    \"\"\"\n109\t    role = response.get(\"role\")\n110\t    if not is_judge_role(role):\n111\t        return []\n112\t    errors: list[str] = []\n113\t    details = response.get(\"details\") or {}\n114\t    if not isinstance(details, dict):\n115\t        details = {}\n116\t    verdict_block = _verdict_field(details)\n117\t    if not verdict_block:\n118\t        return [\"judge_verdict:absent\"]\n119\t    verdict = verdict_block.get(\"verdict\")\n120\t    if verdict not in FEEDBACK_VERDICTS:\n121\t        # ORPHAN-CRITICAL-735 — the arbiter's contract says \"return\n122\t        # details.consensus, OR the uncertainty reason when the consensus\n123\t        # gate cannot be met\", and the deterministic engine it mirrors\n124\t        # emits exactly that non-verdict outcome. The live arbiter obeyed\n125\t        # and this contract burned the claim as invalid:None — fourth\n126\t        # mint-vs-law instance. Uncertainty is a VALID arbitration\n127\t        # outcome, for the arbiter alone, with a reason from the closed\n128\t        # vocabulary; binary judges stay bound to binary verdicts.\n129\t        reason = verdict_block.get(\"uncertainty_reason\")\n130\t        if (\n131\t            role == \"consensus_arbitration\"\n132\t            and verdict is None\n133\t            and reason in CONSENSUS_UNCERTAINTY_REASONS\n134\t        ):\n135\t            pass\n136\t        else:\n137\t            errors.append(f\"judge_verdict.verdict:invalid:{verdict!r}\")\n138\t    tool_id = request.get(\"tool_id\") or verdict_block.get(\"tool_id\")\n139\t    run_id = request.get(\"run_id\") or verdict_block.get(\"run_id\")\n140\t    finding_id = request.get(\"finding_id\") or verdict_block.get(\"finding_id\")\n141\t    if not (tool_id and run_id and finding_id):\n142\t        errors.append(\"judge_verdict:missing_tool_run_or_finding_id\")\n143\t    judge_id = details.get(\"agent_subagent_type\") or verdict_block.get(\"judge_id\")\n144\t    if not judge_id:\n145\t        errors.append(\"judge_verdict:missing_judge_identity\")\n146\t    elif str(judge_id).startswith(\"ci-executor:\"):\n147\t        errors.append(\"judge_verdict:executor_shaped_judge_identity\")\n148\t    return errors\n149\t\n150\t\n151\tdef record_judge_verdict_from_response(\n152\t    *,\n153\t    request: dict[str, Any],\n154\t    response: dict[str, Any],\n155\t    base_dir: str | Path | None = None,\n156\t) -> dict[str, Any] | None:\n157\t    \"\"\"Translate an ACCEPTED judge response into an ai_judge feedback row.\n158\t\n159\t    Returns the persisted row, or None when the envelope role is not a\n160\t    judge role (the bridge is a no-op for non-judge submissions).\n161\t\n162\t    Raises GovernanceError when the response is missing the data the\n163\t    consensus engine requires (tool_id / run_id / finding_id come from\n164\t    the request envelope's optional fields; verdict + severity from\n165\t    the response.details).\n166\t    \"\"\"\n167\t    role = response.get(\"role\")\n168\t    if not is_judge_role(role):\n169\t        return None\n170\t\n171\t    # Y5 (ORPHAN-706) — one contract, two consumers: the executor's\n172\t    # pre-submit gate checks exactly this list and releases instead of\n173\t    # sealing an unfoldable accepted result; the bridge raises on it so a\n174\t    # row that somehow slipped past still fails loudly, never half-folds.\n175\t    contract_errors = validate_judge_response(request=request, response=response)\n176\t    if contract_errors:\n177\t        raise GovernanceError(\n178\t            \"judge bridge contract violation: \" + \"; \".join(contract_errors)\n179\t        )\n180\t\n181\t    details = response.get(\"details\") or {}\n182\t    if not isinstance(details, dict):\n183\t        details = {}\n184\t\n185\t    # ORPHAN-CRITICAL-735 — the arbiter's legitimate non-verdict outcome\n186\t    # folds through the SAME producer, ledger and idempotent escalation_id\n187\t    # as the deterministic engine, so one sweep drains both lanes into one\n188\t    # HUMAN_REQUIRED record. It is NOT a feedback verdict: an uncertain\n189\t    # arbitration must never write an ai_judge row.\n190\t    if role == \"consensus_arbitration\":\n191\t        arb_block = _verdict_field(details)\n192\t        arb_reason = arb_block.get(\"uncertainty_reason\")\n193\t        if arb_block.get(\"verdict\") is None and arb_reason in CONSENSUS_UNCERTAINTY_REASONS:\n194\t            from .feedback_store import record_consensus_uncertainty\n195\t\n196\t            row = record_consensus_uncertainty(\n197\t                tool_id=str(request.get(\"tool_id\") or arb_block.get(\"tool_id\") or \"\"),\n198\t                run_id=str(request.get(\"run_id\") or arb_block.get(\"run_id\") or \"\"),\n199\t                finding_id=str(request.get(\"finding_id\") or arb_block.get(\"finding_id\") or \"\"),\n200\t                group_id=str(request.get(\"judgment_group_id\") or arb_block.get(\"group_id\") or \"\"),\n201\t                reason=str(arb_reason),\n202\t                cycle_id=request.get(\"cycle_id\"),\n203\t                base_dir=base_dir,\n204\t            )\n205\t            return {\"kind\": \"consensus_uncertainty\", **row}\n206\t    verdict_block = _verdict_field(details)\n207\t\n208\t    tool_id = request.get(\"tool_id\") or verdict_block.get(\"tool_id\")\n209\t    run_id = request.get(\"run_id\") or verdict_block.get(\"run_id\")\n210\t    finding_id = request.get(\"finding_id\") or verdict_block.get(\"finding_id\")\n211\t    if not (tool_id and run_id and finding_id):\n212\t        raise GovernanceError(\n213\t            \"judge bridge requires tool_id, run_id, finding_id in request or response.details.verdict; \"\n214\t            f\"got tool_id={tool_id!r}, run_id={run_id!r}, finding_id={finding_id!r}\"\n215\t        )\n216\t\n217\t    verdict = verdict_block.get(\"verdict\")\n218\t    if verdict not in FEEDBACK_VERDICTS:\n219\t _coerce_severity(verdict_block.get(\"severity\") or details.get(\"severity\"))\n223\t    # D1 (Kapalı Döngü) — judge identity. `response[\"agent_id\"]` is the\n224\t    # EXECUTOR's identity judge_id=str(judge_id),\n271\t        # ORPHAN-HIGH-781 — mint-first doctrine, same as judgment_group_id\n272\t        # and finding_fingerprint above: the model the DISPATCHER resolved\n273\t The anchor distinct-model count must not trust a\n276\t        # string the judged agent wrote about itself; the self-report\n277\t        # survives only as the fallback for mock/legacy envelopes that\n278\t        # predate the stamp.\n279\t        model=details.get(\"agent_dispatch_model\") or verdict_block.get(\"model\"),"}]},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"5a4c4f70-f8bc-4bf7-9998-cc5c20f832fa","uuid":"d9e66c00-43dc-4d10-ab2f-54f1ce6cdc83","timestamp":"2026-09-19T15:28:56.991Z","tool_use_result":{"type":"text","file":{"filePath":"/home/gharunner/actions-runner/_work/aquaculture_platform/aquaculture_platform/aria-worktrees/req-AIR-aria-evidence-judge-a4a4db32a408/aria-kernel/aria_kernel/judgment_bridge.py","content":"from .tool_registry import GovernanceError, append_tools_governance, ensure_tools_dir, utc_now\n\n\ndef is_judge_role(role: str | None) -> bool:\n    return role in JUDGE_ROLES\n\n\ndef is_supporting_role(role: str | None) -> bool:\n    return role in SUPPORTING_ROLES\n\n\ndef _coerce_severity(value: Any, *, fallback: str = \"medium\") -> str:\n    \"\"\"feedback_store accepts {low, medium, high, critical}; agent envelope\n    may use uppercase per finding schema. Normalize without rejecting.\"\"\"\n    if not isinstance(value, str):\n        return fallback\n    normalized = value.strip().lower()\n    if normalized in FEEDBACK_SEVERITIES:\n        return normalized\n    if normalized == \"informational\":\n        return \"low\"\n    return fallback\n\n\ndef _verdict_field(details: dict[str, Any]) -> dict[str, Any]:\n    \"\"\"The judge .md prompts shape verdicts under details.verdict (evidence,\n    adversarial) or details.consensus (arbiter). Return whichever exists.\n    \"\"\"\n    if isinstance(details.get(\"verdict\"), dict):\n        return dict(details[\"verdict\"])\n    if isinstance(details.get(\"consensus\"), dict):\n        return dict(details[\"consensus\"])\n    if isinstance(details.get(\"verdict\"), str):\n        # Flat shape (ORPHAN-HIGH-629 sibling): the judge contract file says\n        # BOTH \"Return JSON with: verdict, judge_id, confidence...\" and\n        # \"details.verdict.rationale\". The first live judge run answered\n        # flat, was ACCEPTED by the envelope gate, and dropped HERE with\n        # \"expected verdict ..., got None\" — a verdict lost between two\n        # halves of one document. Honour both spellings; the envelope gate\n        # stays the only authority on validity.\n        return {\n            key: details.get(key)\n            for key in (\n                \"verdict\", \"tool_id\", \"run_id\", \"finding_id\", \"judge_id\",\n                \"model\", \"prompt_hash\", \"confidence\", \"rationale\",\n                \"evidence_refs\", \"judgment_group_id\", \"severity\",\n            )\n            if details.get(key) is not None\n        }\n    return {}\n\n\ndef validate_judge_response(\n    *,\n    request: dict[str, Any],\n    response: dict[str, Any],\n) -> list[str]:\n    \"\"\"Y5 (ORPHAN-706) — the judge output contract, checkable BEFORE accept.\n\n    The second sealed night accepted 12 judge results whose envelopes never\n    carried a verdict the bridge could read (\"expected verdict ..., got\n    None\") — each one an accepted row that could never fold, burning bridge\n    retries toward permanent_fail. This is the SINGLE definition of what a\n    judge response must carry; ``record_judge_verdict_from_response`` raises\n    on exactly this list, and the executor's pre-submit gate releases the\n    claim on it instead of sealing an unfoldable result.\n\n    Returns [] for non-judge roles: the contract binds judges only.\n    \"\"\"\n    role = response.get(\"role\")\n    if not is_judge_role(role):\n        return []\n    errors: list[str] = []\n    details = response.get(\"details\") or {}\n    if not isinstance(details, dict):\n        details = {}\n    verdict_block = _verdict_field(details)\n    if not verdict_block:\n        return [\"judge_verdict:absent\"]\n    verdict = verdict_block.get(\"verdict\")\n    if verdict not in FEEDBACK_VERDICTS:\n        # ORPHAN-CRITICAL-735 — the arbiter's contract says \"return\n        # details.consensus, OR the uncertainty reason when the consensus\n        # gate cannot be met\", and the deterministic engine it mirrors\n        # emits exactly that non-verdict outcome. The live arbiter obeyed\n        # and this contract burned the claim as invalid:None — fourth\n        # mint-vs-law instance. Uncertainty is a VALID arbitration\n        # outcome, for the arbiter alone, with a reason from the closed\n        # vocabulary; binary judges stay bound to binary verdicts.\n        reason = verdict_block.get(\"uncertainty_reason\")\n        if (\n            role == \"consensus_arbitration\"\n            and verdict is None\n            and reason in CONSENSUS_UNCERTAINTY_REASONS\n        ):\n            pass\n        else:\n            errors.append(f\"judge_verdict.verdict:invalid:{verdict!r}\")\n    tool_id = request.get(\"tool_id\") or verdict_block.get(\"tool_id\")\n    run_id = request.get(\"run_id\") or verdict_block.get(\"run_id\")\n    finding_id = request.get(\"finding_id\") or verdict_block.get(\"finding_id\")\n    if not (tool_id and run_id and finding_id):\n        errors.append(\"judge_verdict:missing_tool_run_or_finding_id\")\n    judge_id = details.get(\"agent_subagent_type\") or verdict_block.get(\"judge_id\")\n    if not judge_id:\n        errors.append(\"judge_verdict:missing_judge_identity\")\n    elif str(judge_id).startswith(\"ci-executor:\"):\n        errors.append(\"judge_verdict:executor_shaped_judge_identity\")\n    return errors\n\n\ndef record_judge_verdict_from_response(\n    *,\n    request: dict[str, Any],\n    response: dict[str, Any],\n    base_dir: str | Path | None = None,\n) -> dict[str, Any] | None:\n    \"\"\"Translate an ACCEPTED judge response into an ai_judge feedback row.\n\n    Returns the persisted row, or None when the envelope role is not a\n    judge role (the bridge is a no-op for non-judge submissions).\n\n    Raises GovernanceError when the response is missing the data the\n    consensus engine requires (tool_id / run_id / finding_id come from\n    the request envelope's optional fields; verdict + severity from\n    the response.details).\n    \"\"\"\n    role = response.get(\"role\")\n    if not is_judge_role(role):\n        return None\n\n    # Y5 (ORPHAN-706) — one contract, two consumers: the executor's\n    # pre-submit gate checks exactly this list and releases instead of\n    # sealing an unfoldable accepted result; the bridge raises on it so a\n    # row that somehow slipped past still fails loudly, never half-folds.\n    contract_errors = validate_judge_response(request=request, response=response)\n    if contract_errors:\n        raise GovernanceError(\n            \"judge bridge contract violation: \" + \"; \".join(contract_errors)\n        )\n\n    details = response.get(\"details\") or {}\n    if not isinstance(details, dict):\n        details = {}\n\n    # ORPHAN-CRITICAL-735 — the arbiter's legitimate non-verdict outcome\n    # folds through the SAME producer, ledger and idempotent escalation_id\n    # as the deterministic engine, so one sweep drains both lanes into one\n    # HUMAN_REQUIRED record. It is NOT a feedback verdict: an uncertain\n    # arbitration must never write an ai_judge row.\n    if role == \"consensus_arbitration\":\n        arb_block = _verdict_field(details)\n        arb_reason = arb_block.get(\"uncertainty_reason\")\n        if arb_block.get(\"verdict\") is None and arb_reason in CONSENSUS_UNCERTAINTY_REASONS:\n            from .feedback_store import record_consensus_uncertainty\n\n            row = record_consensus_uncertainty(\n                tool_id=str(request.get(\"tool_id\") or arb_block.get(\"tool_id\") or \"\"),\n                run_id=str(request.get(\"run_id\") or arb_block.get(\"run_id\") or \"\"),\n                finding_id=str(request.get(\"finding_id\") or arb_block.get(\"finding_id\") or \"\"),\n                group_id=str(request.get(\"judgment_group_id\") or arb_block.get(\"group_id\") or \"\"),\n                reason=str(arb_reason),\n                cycle_id=request.get(\"cycle_id\"),\n                base_dir=base_dir,\n            )\n            return {\"kind\": \"consensus_uncertainty\", **row}\n    verdict_block = _verdict_field(details)\n\n    tool_id = request.get(\"tool_id\") or verdict_block.get(\"tool_id\")\n    run_id = request.get(\"run_id\") or verdict_block.get(\"run_id\")\n    finding_id = request.get(\"finding_id\") or verdict_block.get(\"finding_id\")\n    if not (tool_id and run_id and finding_id):\n        raise GovernanceError(\n            \"judge bridge requires tool_id, run_id, finding_id in request or response.details.verdict; \"\n            f\"got tool_id={tool_id!r}, run_id={run_id!r}, finding_id={finding_id!r}\"\n        )\n\n    verdict = verdict_block.get(\"verdict\")\n    if verdict not in FEEDBACK_VERDICTS:\n        raise GovernanceError(\n            f\"judge bridge expected verdict in {FEEDBACK_VERDICTS}, got {verdict!r}\"\n        )\n    severity = _coerce_severity(verdict_block.get(\"severity\") or details.get(\"severity\"))\n    # D1 (Kapalı Döngü) — judge identity. `response[\"agent_id\"]` is the\n    # EXECUTOR's identity (`ci-executor:gha-<run>`), not the judge's: reading\n    # it collapsed every judge drained by one workflow run into a single\n    # voter (consensus structurally impossible) while judges drained by\n    # DIFFERENT runs counted as distinct voters with meaningless identity.\n    # The judge's real identity is the subagent the executor invoked\n    # (`details.agent_subagent_type`, force-stamped by ci_executor); the\n    # mock lane and legacy envelopes carry it as `verdict.judge_id`.\n    judge_id = details.get(\"agent_subagent_type\") or verdict_block.get(\"judge_id\")\n    if not judge_id:\n        raise GovernanceError(\n            \"judge bridge requires details.agent_subagent_type \"\n            \"(or details.verdict.judge_id)\"\n        )\n    if str(judge_id).startswith(\"ci-executor:\"):\n        # Refuse loudly instead of silently repairing: an executor-shaped\n        # identity means the producer regressed, and a repaired row would\n        # hide that while still poisoning per-judge calibration.\n        raise GovernanceError(\n            f\"judge bridge refuses executor-shaped judge identity: {judge_id!r}\"\n        )\n\n    # The unit-interval gate, not a bare isinstance: True is an int and\n    # float(\"nan\") is a float to isinstance, and both would ride a judge\n    # verdict into calibration as if they were probabilities.\n    confidence = confidence_in_unit_interval(verdict_block.get(\"confidence\"))\n\n    note = (\n        verdict_block.get(\"rationale\")\n        or verdict_block.get(\"note\")\n        or response.get(\"rationale\")\n        or \"envelope-routed judge submission\"\n    )\n    if not isinstance(note, str) or not note.strip():\n        note = \"envelope-routed judge submission\"\n\n    raw_evidence_refs = verdict_block.get(\"evidence_refs\") or response.get(\"evidence_refs\") or []\n    evidence_refs = [str(r) for r in raw_evidence_refs if isinstance(r, str) and r.strip()]\n\n    row = record_operator_feedback(\n        tool_id=str(tool_id),\n        run_id=str(run_id),\n        finding_id=str(finding_id),\n        verdict=verdict,\n        severity=severity,\n        note=note,\n        source_type=\"ai_judge\",\n        judge_id=str(judge_id),\n        # ORPHAN-HIGH-781 — mint-first doctrine, same as judgment_group_id\n        # and finding_fingerprint above: the model the DISPATCHER resolved\n        # (`details.agent_dispatch_model`, force-stamped by ci_executor from\n        # the runtime profile) outranks the judge's own verdict.model\n        # self-report. The anchor distinct-model count must not trust a\n        # string the judged agent wrote about itself; the self-report\n        # survives only as the fallback for mock/legacy envelopes that\n        # predate the stamp.\n        model=details.get(\"agent_dispatch_model\") or verdict_block.get(\"model\"),","numLines":240,"startLine":40,"totalLines":388}}}

{"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-5","id":"msg_011CfD33Lk7buhyMykeW32j5","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"text","text":"**Verdict: true_positive** (confidence 0.84). The excerpt alone could not define what the rule's \"allowlist\" and \"tenant-skip rationale\" mean, so I read the adapter source, its tool manifest, the kernel dispatch line, the admin-api guard chain, the service's SSoT `Public` decorator, and the invariant specs; the controller itself was not re-read (a grep for `isPublic` returned lines 32–33 exactly as the excerpt shows).\n\n```json\n{\n  \"$schema\": \"aria/agent-response/v1\",\n  \"request_id\": \"AIR-aria-evidence-judge-a4a4db32a408\",\n  \"claim_id\": \"\",\n  \"agent_id\": \"aria-evidence-judge\",\n  \"role\": \"evidence_judgment\",\n  \"status\": \"submitted\",\n  \"satisfaction_matrix\": [\n    {\n      \"id\": \"verdict\",\n      \"verdict\": \"satisfied\",\n      \"note\": \"true_positive. password-reset.controller.ts:62 opens the decorator block of forgotPassword: @Post('forgot-password') at :63 and @Public() at :64 make it an unauthenticated write route; the class decorators at :43-44 are @ApiTags/@Controller only and @SkipTenantGuard appears nowhere in the file (resetPassword at :98-102 has the same shape). The adapter rule (security-boundary-adapter.ts:210-217) reports exactly this shape unless the file is in the adapter's allowlist input (:99) or carries @SkipTenantGuard (:184). Neither remedy exists in the repository: the tool manifest's default_input carries only roots (security-boundary-adapter.tool.json:22) and the cycle passes default_input verbatim (cycle.py:1312); admin-api-service's APP_GUARD chain (app.module.ts:371, :375, :384) has no TenantGuard and never uses @SkipTenantGuard. The route's public status rests on a LOCAL one-off IS_PUBLIC_KEY/Public declared at :32-33 rather than the service SSoT (decorators/public.decorator.ts:3) that PlatformAdminGuard reads (platform-admin.guard.ts:35, :156-163) — the same state the repository's own public-write precedent forbids (webhook-public-paths.spec.ts:76, :95, BILLING-LOW-004). Live behaviour is correct today because the two strings coincide, so the defect is an unacknowledged, unlinked public write surface, not an auth bypass.\",\n      \"evidence_refs\": [\n \"public-write-endpoint-without-allowlist:apps/admin-api-service/src/auth/password-reset.controller.ts:62\",\n      \"verdict\": \"true_positive\",\n      \"judge_id\": \"aria-evidence-judge\",\n      \"model\": \"claude-opus-5\",\n      \"prompt_hash\": null,\n      \"prompt_hash_note\": \"No hashing tool is available on this route; the executor stamps details.agent_contract_hash at submit.\",\n      \"confidence\": 0.84,\n      \"severity\": \"medium\",\n      \"severity_note\": \"The adapter's 'high' names the risk class (an unenumerated unauthenticated write surface on the most privileged, directly reachable service, platform-admin.guard.ts:105). This endpoint's present runtime exposure is bounded by throttling (:65, :101), auditing (:62, :98), whitelist DTO validation (password-reset.security.spec.ts:66) and a constant enumeration-safe response (:89), so the judged severity is medium; the root-cause fix below is still required.\",\n      \"rationale\": \"What the rule checks (security-boundary-adapter.ts:210-217): a handler or class carrying a write route decorator plus @Public(), with neither @SkipTenantGuard (:184) nor a file entry in the adapter's allowlist input (:99), is reported. What the cited file shows: :62 opens the decorator block of forgotPassword, @Post('forgot-password') at :63 and @Public() at :64 make it an unauthenticated write route; the class decorators at :43-44 are @ApiTags/@Controller only; @SkipTenantGuard appears nowhere in the file; resetPassword at :98-102 has the same shape. Why both remedies the rule names are absent rather than merely unused: the adapter's allowlist is an input the kernel never populates — the tool manifest's default_input carries only roots (security-boundary-adapter.tool.json:22) and the cycle passes default_input verbatim (aria-kernel/aria_kernel/cycle.py:1312) — and @SkipTenantGuard is used nowhere in admin-api-service, whose APP_GUARD chain is PlatformAdminGuard, ThrottlerGuard and PlatformCapabilityGuard (app.module.ts:371, :375, :384) with no TenantGuard, so that marker has no consumer in this service (in services that do run TenantGuard the convention pairs @Public() with @SkipTenantGuard() and a rationale comment, provisioning.controller.ts:44-46; TenantGuard itself already honours isPublic, tenant.guard.ts:50, :111). The public status the guard honours comes from a LOCAL one-off decorator: the file declares its own IS_PUBLIC_KEY/Public at :32-33 instead of importing apps/admin-api-service/src/decorators/public.decorator.ts (:3), which platform-admin.guard.ts:35 names as the key the guard reads ('instead of declaring a local isPublic copy the guard must keep in sync', APA-371); PlatformAdminGuard short-circuits on that key at :156-161. The two strings coincide today, so live behaviour is correct, but nothing links them. The repository's own precedent for an intentionally public write route treats exactly this state as a defect class: tests/invariants/webhook-public-paths.spec.ts:76-95 (BILLING-LOW-004) pins the Stripe webhook's @Public() and at :95 forbids a local 'const Public =' declaration in the file. No equivalent invariant or allowlist entry exists for the password-reset routes: the only tests naming the file are the NATS-facade pin (tenant-provisioning-ssot.spec.ts:751), the MFA exemption keyed on @Public() presence (platform-admin-mfa-ssot.spec.ts:16, :103), the controller's own security spec, which asserts behaviour inside a guard-free test module (password-reset.security.spec.ts:9, :66), and an HMAC allowlist entry (service-identity-canonical-coverage.spec.ts:63) whose stated reason describes token HMAC the controller no longer performs (no crypto import in the file). Downstream consequence of leaving this unacknowledged: the set of unauthenticated mutation routes on admin-api-service is not enumerated anywhere a gate reads, so a further @Public() @Post added to this service is silent, and a drift between the local key and the SSoT key would silently auth-gate account recovery for every tenant without a test failing. What does NOT make this false: the endpoint is legitimately public and well protected — throttled (:65, :101), audited (:62, :98), DTO-validated with whitelist/forbidNonWhitelisted (password-reset.security.spec.ts:66), enumeration-safe (:89; spec :127), delegated to auth-service public NATS commands (:72) — so the rule is not claiming the route should require auth; it is claiming the public-write status lacks an explicit, machine-readable acknowledgment, and that claim holds. Recommended root-cause fix: (1) Tier 1 — delete the local IS_PUBLIC_KEY/Public at :31-33 and import Public from apps/admin-api-service/src/decorators/public.decorator.ts so the key cannot drift; (2) Tier 3 — add the explicit acknowledgment where the repository's precedent puts it: an invariant spec modelled on webhook-public-paths.spec.ts asserting @Public() plus @ThrottlePasswordReset() on both @Post handlers and forbidding a local Public declaration, and/or an allowlist entry with rationale in security-boundary-adapter.tool.json default_input so the adapter's own acknowledgment channel is exercised instead of empty. Confidence 0.84: the rule condition is verified verbatim in the cited lines; the residual uncertainty is only whether the missing acknowledgment, with correct live behaviour, is treated as actionable — the repository's BILLING-LOW-004 and APA-371 precedents say it is.\",\n      \"evidence_refs\": [\n        \"apps/admin-api-service/src/auth/password-reset.controller.ts:32\",\n        \"apps/admin-api-service/src/auth/password-reset.controller.ts:33\",\n        \"apps/admin-api-service/src/auth/password-reset.controller.ts:62\",\n        \"apps/admin-api-service/src/auth/password-reset.controller.ts:63\",\n        \"apps/admin-api-service/src/auth/password-reset.controller.ts:64\",\n        \"apps/admin-api-service/src/auth/password-reset.controller.ts:98\",\n        \"apps/admin-api-service/src/auth/password-reset.controller.ts:100\",\n        \"tools/aria-adapters/security-boundary-adapter.ts:99\",\n        \"tools/aria-adapters/security-boundary-adapter.ts:184\",\n        \"tools/aria-adapters/security-boundary-adapter.ts:210\",\n        \"tools/aria-adapters/security-boundary-adapter.tool.json:22\",\n        \"aria-kernel/aria_kernel/cycle.py:1312\",\n        \"apps/admin-api-service/src/app.module.ts:371\",\n        \"apps/admin-api-service/src/app.module.ts:375\",\n        \"apps/admin-api-service/src/app.module.ts:384\",\n        \"apps/admin-api-service/src/guards/platform-admin.guard.ts:35\",\n        \"apps/admin-api-service/src/guards/platform-admin.guard.ts:156\",\n        \"apps/admin-api-service/src/decorators/public.decorator.ts:3\",\n        \"tests/invariants/webhook-public-paths.spec.ts:76\",\n        \"tests/invariants/webhook-public-paths.spec.ts:95\"\n      ],\n      \"judgment_group_id\": null\n    },\n    \"excerpt_handling\": \"The excerpt (lines 1-174) was used as the primary source for the controller. Additional files were read because the excerpt cannot define the rule's allowlist/tenant-skip semantics (adapter source, tool manifest, kernel cycle dispatch), nor whether admin-api-service runs a tenant guard (app.module.ts, platform-admin.guard.ts), nor whether the repository already acknowledges this public write surface elsewhere (tests/invariants, the controller's security spec).\",\n    \"explanation\": {\n      \"what_must_be_done\": \"Decide whether the adapter's claim — this public write endpoint has neither an allowlist entry nor a tenant-skip rationale — is true at commit 1e6e99f7e using only repository content, and name the root-cause fix if it is.\",\n      \"why_it_matters\": \"@Public() removes the entire guard chain from a mutation route. Every such route on admin-api-service is unauthenticated attack surface on the platform's most privileged service; the rule exists so each one is explicitly enumerated and reviewed rather than discovered later.\",\n      \"what_breaks_if_skipped\": \"Without an enumerated acknowledgment, the next @Public() @Post added to admin-api is silent to every gate, and the local one-off IS_PUBLIC_KEY copy in this file can drift from the SSoT key PlatformAdminGuard reads — divergence would auth-gate account recovery for every tenant with no failing test.\",\n      \"downstream_surface\": \"POST /auth/forgot-password and POST /auth/reset-password on admin-api-service (public account-recovery routes), PlatformAdminGuard's isPublic short-circuit, the security-boundary-adapter allowlist channel, and tests/invariants where the repository records intentional public write routes (BILLING-LOW-004 precedent).\",\n      \"evidence_that_proves_it\": \"password-reset.controller.ts:62-65 and :98-101 app.module.ts:371/:375/:384 (no TenantGuard in admin-api), platform-admin.guard.ts:35/:156-161 + decorators/public.decorator.ts:3 (the key the guard reads), webhook-public-paths.spec.ts:76-95 (repository precedent that forbids this state for a public write route).\"\n    }\n
